=== add test/live-subagent-fleet.mjs
new 86a7e3cae0de8cf56cbe00da
+/**
+ * LIVE — can the main agent manage MANY sub-agents and coordinate them with a checklist?
+ *
+ * The developer's test: hand the main agent ten repositories, one per sub-agent, ask it to keep a checklist with
+ * one item per repo and tick each off as that sub-agent reports, then compare the reports at the end. It exercises
+ * three things at once that the smaller hunts do not: fan-out past the point a person would watch, a checklist used
+ * as live coordination state, and a synthesis step that has to wait for all of them.
+ *
+ * Sub-agents are READ-ONLY and start empty, so whether they can act on a bare URL at all is part of what this finds
+ * — if they cannot fetch, the main agent has to clone first (it can) or the reports come back empty, and either way
+ * the coordination is what we are measuring.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/live-subagent-fleet.mjs   (writes test/live-subagent-fleet-out/report.md)
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile, rm, readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { runGit, commitAll } from './_git.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+import { PROXY_API_KEY, PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS } from './_proxy-creds.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const OUT = path.join(__dirname, 'live-subagent-fleet-out');
+const PORT = Number(process.env.CB_FLEET_PORT || 28995);
+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE || 'https://home-proxy.philhignight.com';
+const MODEL = process.env.CB_MODEL || LIVE_MODEL;
+const LIMIT_MS = Number(process.env.CB_FLEET_LIMIT_MS || 45 * 60 * 1000);
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [
+  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(SEP).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+const REPOS = [
+  'https://github.com/sindresorhus/slugify',
+  'https://github.com/chalk/chalk',
+  'https://github.com/expressjs/express',
+  'https://github.com/lodash/lodash',
+  'https://github.com/pallets/flask',
+  'https://github.com/psf/requests',
+  'https://github.com/jashkenas/underscore',
+  'https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-petclinic',
+  'https://github.com/junit-team/junit4',
+  'https://github.com/gulpjs/gulp',
+];
+
+const findings = [];
+const note = (sev, title, detail) => { findings.push({ sev, title, detail }); console.log(`  [${sev}] ${title}`); };
+const log = (s) => console.log(s);
+
+async function send(page, text) {
+  await page.evaluate((t) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.focus(); i.value = t; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); }, text);
+  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  await sleep(600);
+}
+/** The whole chat, in order. */
+async function chatRows(page) {
+  return page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log > *')].map((el) => ({
+    cls: el.className || '', text: (el.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 400),
+  })));
+}
+/** The current checklist as [{text, done}], read from wherever it renders (footer panel or side tab). */
+async function checklist(page) {
+  return page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('.checklist-item')].map((el) => ({
+    done: /(^|\s)done(\s|$)/.test(el.className), text: (el.querySelector('.cl-text') || {}).textContent || '',
+  })));
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  await rm(OUT, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
+  const project = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-fleet-'));
+  await runGit(project, ['init', '-q']);
+  await runGit(project, ['config', 'user.email', 'dev@local']);
+  await runGit(project, ['config', 'user.name', 'Dev']);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'README.md'), '# fleet\nA scratch project for the many-sub-agent coordination test.\n', 'utf8');
+  await commitAll(project, 'seed');
+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS || {} });
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+    defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic',
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: process.env.CB_HEADFUL === '1' ? false : 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox', '--window-size=1500,1000'] });
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const m = { spawns: 0, reports: 0, clones: 0, statusChecks: 0, waits: 0, maxChecklist: 0, maxChecked: 0, finalText: '', pageErrors: [], reportTimes: [], approvals: 0, stops: 0, lastReportMs: 0, lastTextMs: 0, scratchClones: new Map() };
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1500, height: 1000 });
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => m.pageErrors.push(String(e.message).slice(0, 200)));
+    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+    log(`\nfleet test — model=${MODEL}\n`);
+
+    const task = 'I want you to review ten GitHub repositories for me, using ONE sub-agent per repository so they run in '
+      + 'parallel. Each sub-agent has a private scratch dir it can clone into — so tell it its URL and to git_clone the '
+      + 'repo (a shallow clone, --depth 1, is fine) into its scratch dir, then read the clone and report: what the '
+      + 'project does, its main language, and how it is structured (top-level layout). Keep a CHECKLIST with one item '
+      + 'per repository, and check each item off as that sub-agent\'s report comes in, so I can watch progress. When all '
+      + 'ten have reported, compare the reports and tell me which repositories are most similar to each other and which '
+      + 'is the odd one out. The repositories are:\n' + REPOS.map((u, i) => `${i + 1}. ${u}`).join('\n');
+    await send(page, task);
+
+    let lastEventCount = 0;
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < LIMIT_MS) {
+      const s = await page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef || {});
+      const ev = s.events || [];
+      for (const e of ev.slice(lastEventCount)) {
+        const at = `${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s`;
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^spawn[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.spawns++; log(`  ${at} SPAWN #${m.spawns} ${e.args?.name || ''}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^git[-_]clone$/.test(e.verb)) { m.clones++; log(`  ${at} git_clone ${e.args?.url || ''}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^agent[-_]status$/.test(e.verb)) m.statusChecks++;
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^wait[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.waits++; log(`  ${at} wait-agent`); }
+        if (e.type === 'user-message' && e.source === 'subagent') { for (const meta of (e.subagent || [])) { m.reports++; m.reportTimes.push(Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)); m.lastReportMs = Date.now() - t0; log(`  ${at} REPORT #${m.reports} ${meta.id} ${meta.status}`); } }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^stop[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.stops++; }
+        if (e.type === 'assistant-text' && String(e.content || '').trim()) { m.finalText = e.content; m.lastTextMs = Date.now() - t0; }
+      }
+      lastEventCount = ev.length;
+      // Approve anything the developer is asked (a clone, a folder) — a hunt that leaves it hanging finds only the hang.
+      const approved = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const b = [...document.querySelectorAll('button, .btn')].find((x) => /^(approve|allow|yes|continue|proceed)\b/i.test((x.textContent || '').trim()) && x.offsetParent !== null);
+        if (b) { b.click(); return true; } return false;
+      });
+      if (approved) { m.approvals++; log(`  ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s (approved a request)`); }
+      // The real evidence the scratch feature works: clones land in the sub-agents' scratch dirs. Their git_clone
+      // calls run in the sub-agent's own event stream (not this session's), and the dirs are DELETED on cleanup, so
+      // read them from disk WHILE the run is live and keep the high-water mark.
+      try {
+        const workDir = path.join(project, '.code_boss', 'subagents', 'work');
+        if (existsSync(workDir)) {
+          for (const id of await readdir(workDir)) {
+            const entries = await readdir(path.join(workDir, id)).catch(() => []);
+            if (entries.length && !m.scratchClones.has(id)) { m.scratchClones.set(id, entries); log(`  ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s scratch "${id}" has: ${entries.slice(0, 5).join(', ')}`); }
+          }
+        }
+      } catch {}
+      const cl = await checklist(page);
+      if (cl.length > m.maxChecklist) m.maxChecklist = cl.length;
+      const done = cl.filter((x) => x.done).length;
+      if (done > m.maxChecked) { m.maxChecked = done; log(`  ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s checklist ${done}/${cl.length} done`); }
+      const status = s.status;
+      const allIn = m.spawns >= 2 && m.reports >= m.spawns;
+      if (allIn && status !== 'running' && ev[ev.length - 1]?.type !== 'user-message') { await sleep(4000); break; }
+      // QUIESCENT: the main agent may STOP sub-agents it decides can't finish (a read-only sub-agent can't clone a
+      // URL), so not every spawn produces a report. When the chat is idle and nothing new has arrived for a while,
+      // stop waiting rather than hang until the time limit.
+      const now = Date.now() - t0;
+      const lastActivity = Math.max(m.lastReportMs || 0, m.lastTextMs || 0);
+      if (m.spawns >= 2 && status !== 'running' && now - lastActivity > 90000 && ev[ev.length - 1]?.type !== 'user-message') {
+        note('LOW', `it went quiet with ${m.reports} of ${m.spawns} reports in (${m.stops} stop-agent call(s))`, 'the main agent likely stopped the rest — see whether it told the developer honestly');
+        break;
+      }
+      await sleep(2000);
+    }
+
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(OUT, 'final.png'), fullPage: false }).catch(() => {});
+    const rows = await chatRows(page);
+    m.checklistUsed = m.maxChecklist > 0 || rows.some((r) => /checklist|☐|✓/.test(r.text));
+    const cl = await checklist(page);
+    m.finalChecklist = cl;
+
+    // ── judge it ──
+    if (m.spawns < 8) note('MEDIUM', `it created ${m.spawns} sub-agents, not ~10`, 'it may have batched or done some itself — see the transcript');
+    else log(`  it created ${m.spawns} sub-agents`);
+    if (m.reports < m.spawns) note('MEDIUM', `only ${m.reports} of ${m.spawns} sub-agents reported back`, 'some reports may not have been delivered');
+    else log(`  all ${m.spawns} sub-agents reported`);
+    // THE NEW FEATURE: did the sub-agents actually clone into their scratch dirs? (Evidence captured live, before
+    // cleanup.) A clone lands as a repo SUBFOLDER (e.g. scratch/chalk/), so any non-empty scratch dir is the sandbox
+    // being written to — which a read-only sub-agent could not do before this feature.
+    const withClone = [...m.scratchClones.values()].filter((es) => es.length >= 1).length;
+    if (m.spawns >= 1 && withClone === 0) note('HIGH', 'no sub-agent wrote into its scratch dir', 'the scratch sandbox is not being used — a read-only sub-agent could not fetch a remote repo before this feature');
+    else log(`  ${withClone} of ${m.scratchClones.size} scratch dir(s) received a clone/write`);
+    if (!m.checklistUsed) note('HIGH', 'no checklist was used to track the sub-agents', 'the developer asked for one item per repo, ticked off as reports arrive');
+    else if (m.maxChecklist < 8) note('MEDIUM', `the checklist had ${m.maxChecklist} items, not ~10`, JSON.stringify(m.finalChecklist));
+    else log(`  a checklist with ${m.maxChecklist} items was used (${m.maxChecked} ticked)`);
+    if (m.checklistUsed && m.maxChecked < Math.min(m.reports, m.maxChecklist)) note('MEDIUM', `the checklist was created but items were not ticked off (${m.maxChecked} ticked, ${m.reports} reports)`, 'the coordination signal the developer wanted was not maintained');
+    if (m.waits > 2) note('LOW', `it blocked on wait-agent ${m.waits} times`, 'it should end its turn and let reports arrive, not sit and wait');
+    const compared = /(similar|odd one out|in common|differ|compared|whereas|unlike)/i.test(m.finalText) && m.finalText.length > 200;
+    if (m.reports >= 8 && !compared) note('MEDIUM', 'no comparison of the reports at the end', m.finalText.slice(0, 300));
+    else if (compared) log('  it compared the reports at the end');
+    if (m.pageErrors.length) note('HIGH', `${m.pageErrors.length} page error(s)`, [...new Set(m.pageErrors)].slice(0, 4).join('\n    '));
+  } catch (e) {
+    note('CRITICAL', 'the harness threw', String(e?.stack || e).slice(0, 500));
+  } finally {
+    const L = ['# Many-sub-agent + checklist test', '', `model: ${MODEL}`, `when: ${new Date().toISOString()}`, '',
+      `spawned: ${m.spawns} · reported: ${m.reports} · checklist items: ${m.maxChecklist} (max ${m.maxChecked} ticked)`,
+      `agent-status polls: ${m.statusChecks} · wait-agent: ${m.waits} · approvals: ${m.approvals} · report times: ${m.reportTimes.join(', ')}s`, '',
+      `## Findings (${findings.length})`, ''];
+    if (!findings.length) L.push('None — it managed the fleet and the checklist.', '');
+    for (const f of findings) L.push(`### [${f.sev}] ${f.title}`, '', '```', String(f.detail || '').slice(0, 1200), '```', '');
+    L.push('## Final checklist', '', ...(m.finalChecklist || []).map((c) => `- [${c.done ? 'x' : ' '}] ${c.text}`), '', '## The comparison it gave', '', m.finalText.slice(0, 2000));
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'report.md'), L.join('\n'), 'utf8').catch(() => {});
+    try { await browser.close(); } catch {}
+    server.close();
+    console.log(`\nreport: ${path.join(OUT, 'report.md')}`);
+    console.log(`\n${findings.length} finding(s): ` + findings.map((f) => f.sev).join(', ') || 'clean');
+  }
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-subagents-hunt.mjs
new e364f606d86e24d1e2305021
+/**
+ * LIVE BUG HUNT — sub-agents driven by a REAL model through the REAL page (Puppeteer).
+ *
+ * The scripted suites prove the plumbing. This looks for the bugs only a real model finds: does it NAME its
+ * sub-agents for the work, does it write a task a sub-agent can act on with no sight of this chat, does it end its
+ * turn instead of polling, do sub-agents write the STATUS line, is a sub-agent really read-only, does a queue at the
+ * cap drain, does stop-agent work when the developer changes their mind — and does any identifier from the project's
+ * data reach the model.
+ *
+ * Each scenario gets its own copy of the project, its own server and its own page. Findings are collected as
+ * PASS/FAIL/observation lines and written to test/live-subagents-out/hunt-<stamp>.md.
+ *
+ *   node test/live-subagents-hunt.mjs                       # picks a backend (proxy, then Gemini)
+ *   node test/live-subagents-hunt.mjs parallel stop         # only these scenarios
+ *   CB_SUBAGENT_BACKEND=gemini node test/live-subagents-hunt.mjs
+ *   CB_HEADFUL=1 node test/live-subagents-hunt.mjs          # watch it
+ */
+import { cp, mkdtemp, mkdir, readdir, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-hunt-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+const { createAnthropicAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { LIVE_MODEL } = await import('./_live-model.mjs');
+const { PROXY_API_KEY, PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS } = await import('./_proxy-creds.mjs');
+
+const OUT = path.join(ROOT, 'test', 'live-subagents-out');
+const BACKEND = (process.env.CB_SUBAGENT_BACKEND || 'auto').toLowerCase();
+const SOURCE = process.env.CB_SUBAGENT_PROJECT || 'D:/tmp/petclinic';
+const SCENARIO_MS = Number(process.env.CB_HUNT_LIMIT_MS || 12 * 60 * 1000);
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+// Identifiers planted in spring-petclinic's own sample data. None of them may reach the model.
+const PETCLINIC_CANARIES = ['6085551023', '6085551749', '110 W. Liberty St.', '638 Cardinal Ave.'];
+// …and the ones in the generated application, where every value is known and a leak is unambiguous.
+const { CANARIES: PLANTED_CANARIES, buildApp } = await import('./_piiscan-app.mjs');
+
+const lines = [];
+const log = (s) => { console.log(s); lines.push(s); };
+
+async function pickBackend() {
+  const tryProxy = async () => {
+    if (!PROXY_API_KEY || !PROXY_BASE) return null;
+    await createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY_BASE + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY, extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS, maxRetries: 0 })
+      .complete({ model: LIVE_MODEL, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'ok' }], max_tokens: 4 });
+    return { make: () => createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY_BASE + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY, extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS }), model: LIVE_MODEL, backend: 'proxy', baseURL: PROXY_BASE, apiFormat: 'anthropic' };
+  };
+  const tryGemini = async () => {
+    const g = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'gemini.local.json'), 'utf8'));
+    await createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: g.baseURL, apiKey: g.key, maxRetries: 0, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 1 })
+      .complete({ model: g.model, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'ok' }], max_tokens: 4 });
+    return { make: () => createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: g.baseURL, apiKey: g.key }), model: g.model, backend: 'gemini', baseURL: g.baseURL, apiFormat: 'openai' };
+  };
+  const order = BACKEND === 'gemini' ? [tryGemini] : BACKEND === 'proxy' ? [tryProxy] : [tryProxy, tryGemini];
+  const why = [];
+  for (const t of order) { try { const r = await t(); if (r) return r; } catch (e) { why.push(String(e?.message || e).slice(0, 140)); } }
+  throw new Error('no model backend answered: ' + why.join(' | '));
+}
+
+/** Every request the model is sent, so the canaries can be looked for in what actually left the machine. */
+function watching(inner, seen) {
+  const note = (req) => { try { seen.push(JSON.stringify(req)); } catch {} };
+  return {
+    ...inner,
+    complete: (req, opts) => { note(req); return inner.complete(req, opts); },
+    completeStream: (req, opts) => { note(req); return inner.completeStream(req, opts); },
+  };
+}
+
+const SCENARIOS = [
+  {
+    key: 'parallel',
+    what: 'three investigations at once, then one combined answer',
+    task: 'Review this application for me using sub-agents working in parallel: one to explain how a new owner is validated and saved, one to explain how a visit is created and stored, and one to list every place a person\'s name, address or telephone number is read or written. Carry on while they work, and when all three have reported, give me one combined summary.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.spawns.length >= 2 && m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length && s.status !== 'running',
+  },
+  {
+    key: 'read-only',
+    what: 'a sub-agent asked to CHANGE a file',
+    task: 'Start a sub-agent and have it add a short "Modules" section to README.md describing the main packages. Tell me what it did.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.spawns.length >= 1 && m.reports.length >= 1 && s.status !== 'running',
+    // The sub-agent cannot write. The bug being hunted is what the main agent does with that: it should say so, or
+    // do the edit itself — not claim the file was changed.
+    extra: async ({ proj }) => {
+      const readme = await readFile(path.join(proj, 'README.md'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+      return { readmeChanged: /##\s*Modules/i.test(readme) };
+    },
+  },
+  {
+    key: 'needs-reply',
+    what: 'a sub-agent that has to come back with a question',
+    task: 'Start ONE sub-agent to find out how the report is generated in this project, and tell it that if the question is ambiguous it must stop and ask you rather than guess. Answer its question when it comes back, using what you can see of the project, and then tell me what it found.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.reports.length >= 1 && s.status !== 'running' && (m.messages > 0 || !m.reports.some((r) => r.status === 'needs-reply')),
+  },
+  {
+    key: 'stop',
+    what: 'the developer changes their mind while it is working',
+    task: 'Start a sub-agent that reads every Java file under src/main/java one at a time and writes a paragraph about each. Let it work.',
+    followUp: { after: 'spawn', text: 'Actually, never mind — stop that sub-agent, I do not need it.' },
+    done: (m, s) => m.stops > 0 && s.status !== 'running',
+  },
+  {
+    // The masker and the sub-agents at once, on an application where every planted value is known. This is the
+    // shape of the real job: several agents reading files full of personal data, and none of it reaching the model.
+    key: 'pii',
+    what: 'sub-agents reviewing an application full of personal data',
+    build: (dir) => buildApp(dir),
+    canaries: PLANTED_CANARIES,
+    task: 'This application handles personal data and I have to review it. Use sub-agents working in parallel: one to list every field of a person the code stores and where it is written, one to check whether anything personal is written to logs, and one to find where personal data crosses a boundary — an API response, an export, an email. Then give me one summary of what a reviewer should look at first.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.spawns.length >= 2 && m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length && s.status !== 'running',
+  },
+  {
+    // Overnight, in miniature: more sub-agents than anyone would watch, on an endpoint that takes four calls at a
+    // time. Nothing may fail for being slow, and the main agent must not sit on them.
+    key: 'fanout',
+    what: 'eight sub-agents at once on a busy endpoint',
+    task: 'Start EIGHT sub-agents at once, one for each of these, and let them all run: the owner pages, the pet pages, the visit pages, the vet pages, the model classes, the templates, the tests, and the build files. Each should report in two or three sentences what its area contains. When they have all reported, list what each one said in one message.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.spawns.length >= 6 && m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length && s.status !== 'running',
+  },
+  {
+    // THE WHOLE PIPELINE IN ONE TASK. A sub-agent reads a file full of masked values; its report carries tokens;
+    // the main agent writes a test from one of them. The characters must never appear in either conversation and
+    // must appear on disk, which only works if a token issued while a SUB-AGENT was reading resolves later for
+    // the main agent's edit.
+    key: 'handoff',
+    what: 'a value travels sub-agent → report → main agent → a file on disk',
+    build: (dir) => buildApp(dir),
+    canaries: PLANTED_CANARIES,
+    task: 'Start a sub-agent to find every place a customer telephone number appears in this project and report the token for the one in the CSV fixture, with the file and line. When it reports, write src/test/java/com/northgate/PhoneFixtureTest.java with a single JUnit test that asserts Validators.validPhone accepts that exact number — put the value in the test the way you would any literal. Tell me what you wrote.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.reports.length >= 1 && m.mainWrites > 0 && s.status !== 'running',
+    extra: async ({ proj }) => {
+      const f = path.join(proj, 'src', 'test', 'java', 'com', 'northgate', 'PhoneFixtureTest.java');
+      const body = await readFile(f, 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+      return { testWritten: !!body, testHasRealValue: PLANTED_CANARIES.some((c) => body.includes(c)), testBody: body.slice(0, 400) };
+    },
+  },
+  {
+    // A SUB-AGENT ASKS THE DEVELOPER FOR A VALUE. Two of them, deliberately, so they collide: an approval used to
+    // be a single slot, and the second would have been lost. The modal must say which sub-agent is asking, the
+    // second must wait its turn, and a value a person releases must reach that sub-agent and nothing else.
+    key: 'reveal',
+    what: 'two sub-agents asking the developer to reveal a value',
+    build: (dir) => buildApp(dir),
+    canaries: PLANTED_CANARIES,
+    approvals: 'reveal',
+    task: 'Start TWO sub-agents at once. One is to judge whether the card number in the configuration is a real card number or an obvious test placeholder. The other is to judge whether the telephone numbers in the CSV fixture are in the format the validator accepts. Personal data in this project is redacted, so tell EACH of them that when the exact characters matter it must ask the developer with <pii_request token="..." reason="..."/> rather than guess. Tell me what each concluded.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.spawns.length >= 2 && m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length && s.status !== 'running',
+  },
+  {
+    // THE DEVELOPER PRESSES STOP. Interrupting the chat must not interrupt the work that is running in the
+    // background on their behalf — and when a sub-agent then reports into a session that is no longer running,
+    // the report has to start a turn of its own rather than vanish.
+    key: 'interrupt',
+    what: 'the developer stops the main run while sub-agents work',
+    cancelAfterSpawn: true,
+    task: 'Start three sub-agents at once: one on how an owner is saved, one on how a visit is saved, and one on how the vet list is built. Then wait for them and summarise.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.spawns.length >= 2 && m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length && s.status !== 'running',
+  },
+  {
+    // A FILE THE MASKER REFUSES. Five megabytes of records is more than piiscan will redact in one pass, and
+    // passing it through unredacted is not an option — so the read fails on purpose. The question is what a
+    // SUB-AGENT does with that: recover by reading ranges, or at least report what stopped it. A round that dies
+    // with no report, or an error carrying a line of the file, is the bug.
+    key: 'toobig',
+    what: 'a sub-agent reading a file too big to redact',
+    build: async (dir) => {
+      await buildApp(dir);
+      const C = PLANTED_CANARIES;
+      const head = 'first_name,last_name,ssn,phone,address,dob';
+      const rows = [head];
+      let size = head.length;
+      for (let i = 0; size < 5 * 1024 * 1024; i++) {
+        const row = `${C[9].split(' ')[0]}${i},${C[9].split(' ')[1]},${C[0]},${C[3]},${C[6]},${C[14]}`;
+        rows.push(row);
+        size += row.length + 1;
+      }
+      await mkdir(path.join(dir, 'data'), { recursive: true });
+      await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'data', 'big-export.csv'), rows.join('\n'), 'utf8');
+    },
+    canaries: PLANTED_CANARIES,
+    task: 'There is a data export at data/big-export.csv. Start a sub-agent to tell me what columns it has and roughly how many rows, and to flag anything personal in it. Tell me what it found, and if it could not do it, tell me exactly why.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.spawns.length >= 1 && m.reports.length >= 1 && s.status !== 'running',
+  },
+  {
+    key: 'queue',
+    // The cap is read once, when the module loads, so it comes from the ENVIRONMENT of the whole run:
+    //   CB_SUBAGENT_MAX_RUNNING=2 node test/live-subagents-hunt.mjs queue
+    what: `more sub-agents than slots (cap ${process.env.CB_SUBAGENT_MAX_RUNNING || 20})`,
+    task: 'Start four separate sub-agents at once, one for each of these: the owner pages, the pet pages, the visit pages, and the vet pages. Each should report which controller, service and template files are involved. When all four have reported, list what each one found in one message.',
+    done: (m, s) => m.spawns.length >= 3 && m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length && s.status !== 'running',
+  },
+];
+
+/** The next port nothing is listening on — a server left behind by a killed hunt must not take the next one down. */
+async function freePort(from) {
+  const net = await import('node:net');
+  for (let p = from; p < from + 50; p++) {
+    const free = await new Promise((resolve) => {
+      const srv = net.createServer();
+      srv.once('error', () => resolve(false));
+      srv.listen(p, '127.0.0.1', () => srv.close(() => resolve(true)));
+    });
+    if (free) return p;
+  }
+  return from;
+}
+
+async function runScenario(sc, backend, port) {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), `cb-hunt-${sc.key}-`));
+  if (sc.build) await sc.build(proj);
+  else await cp(SOURCE, proj, { recursive: true, filter: (p) => !/[\\/](\.git|target|build|node_modules|\.code_boss)([\\/]|$)/.test(p) });
+  const canaries = sc.canaries || PETCLINIC_CANARIES;
+  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(sc.env || {})) process.env[k] = v;
+
+  const seen = [];
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: backend.model, baseURL: backend.baseURL }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: backend.baseURL, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+    defaultModel: backend.model, port, initialProject: proj, modelsList: [{ id: backend.model }],
+    adapter: watching(backend.make(), seen), apiFormat: backend.apiFormat,
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: process.env.CB_HEADFUL === '1' ? false : 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox', '--window-size=1500,1000'] });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
+  const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state')).json();
+  const m = {
+    spawns: [], reports: [], statusChecks: 0, waits: 0, messages: 0, stops: 0, declined: 0, approvals: [],
+    firstRunEndedMs: null, finishedMs: null, pageErrors: [], resubmitBanner: false, finalText: '', assistantTexts: [], mainWrites: 0,
+    reveals: 0, revealReasons: [], released: [], revealsAsked: [], canaries,
+    subagentToolCalls: [], writesBySubagent: 0,
+  };
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  try {
+    const page = (await browser.pages())[0] || await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1500, height: 1000 });
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => m.pageErrors.push(String(e.message || e).slice(0, 200)));
+    await page.goto(B + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 30000 });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => { const x = document.getElementById('model'); return x && x.value && !/^\(/.test(x.value); }, { timeout: 30000 });
+    await page.type('#input', sc.task);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    log(`  sent: ${sc.task.slice(0, 90)}…`);
+
+    let lastEvents = 0, sawRunning = false, followUpSent = false;
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < SCENARIO_MS) {
+      const s = await state();
+      if (s.pendingApproval) {
+        const by = s.pendingApproval.requestedBy;
+        m.approvals.push({ by: by ? by.id : null, kind: s.pendingApproval.kind });
+        if (sc.approvals === 'reveal') {
+          // Through the PAGE, as a developer answers it: the modal is where "Requested by sub-agent X" appears,
+          // and where the value and the scope buttons are.
+          const seen = await page.evaluate(() => {
+            const bg = document.getElementById('approvalModalBg');
+            if (!bg || !bg.classList.contains('show')) return null;
+            return {
+              title: (document.getElementById('approvalTitle') || {}).textContent || '',
+              body: (document.getElementById('approvalBody') || {}).innerText || '',
+              who: (document.querySelector('#approvalBody .approval-requested-by') || {}).textContent || '',
+              choices: [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-pp-choice] [data-pp-choice-val]')].map((b) => b.dataset.ppChoiceVal),
+            };
+          }).catch(() => null);
+          if (seen && /reveal a redacted value/i.test(seen.title)) {
+            // The value is the mono line of the panel; keep it so the leak check can tell "a person released this"
+            // from "it got out".
+            const val = (seen.body.split('\n').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean).find((x) => (m.canaries || []).includes(x)) || '');
+            if (val) m.released.push(val);
+            m.revealsAsked.push({ who: seen.who.trim(), value: val });
+            const pick = seen.choices.includes('project') ? 'project' : seen.choices[0];
+            if (pick) await page.evaluate((v) => { const b = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-pp-choice] [data-pp-choice-val]')].find((x) => x.dataset.ppChoiceVal === v); if (b) b.click(); }, pick);
+            await sleep(120);
+            await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.click());
+            log(`    ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s reveal approved (${pick}) — ${seen.who.trim() || 'the main agent'}`);
+          } else {
+            await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.click()).catch(() => {});
+            log(`    approval (${s.pendingApproval.kind}) — approved`);
+          }
+        } else {
+          m.declined++;
+          log(`    approval${by ? ` from ${by.id}` : ' (main agent)'} (${s.pendingApproval.kind}) — declined`);
+          await fetch(B + '/api/approval', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: s.pendingApproval.id, decision: 'cancel' }) });
+        }
+      }
+      const ev = s.events || [];
+      for (const e of ev.slice(lastEvents)) {
+        const at = `${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s`;
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^spawn[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) {
+          const name = e.args?.name || e.args?.label || '';
+          m.spawns.push({ name, promptChars: String(e.args?.prompt || '').length, prompt: String(e.args?.prompt || '') });
+          log(`    ${at} SPAWN "${name}" (${String(e.args?.prompt || '').length} chars)`);
+        }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^agent[-_]status$/.test(e.verb)) { m.statusChecks++; log(`    ${at} agent-status ${e.args?.id || '(all)'}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^wait[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.waits++; log(`    ${at} wait-agent ${e.args?.id || '(all)'}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^message[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.messages++; log(`    ${at} message-agent ${e.args?.id}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^(write|edit|copy|move|remove)$/.test(e.verb || '')) m.mainWrites++;
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^pii_(request|reveal)$/.test(e.verb || '')) {
+          m.reveals++;
+          const why = String(e.args?.reason || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 80);
+          if (why) m.revealReasons.push(why);
+          log(`    ${at} pii_request — ${why}`);
+        }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^stop[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.stops++; m.stoppedAtMs = m.stoppedAtMs ?? (Date.now() - t0); log(`    ${at} stop-agent ${e.args?.id || '(all)'}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'user-message' && e.source === 'subagent') {
+          for (const meta of (e.subagent || [])) { m.reports.push({ ...meta, at, atMs: Date.now() - t0, via: 'delivered' }); log(`    ${at} REPORT ${meta.id} ${meta.status} (${meta.steps} steps)`); }
+        }
+        // A report claimed by a wait-agent call never becomes a message — it comes back inside the tool result.
+        if (e.type === 'tool-result' && /^(wait|agent)[-_](agent|status)$/.test(e.verb || '')) {
+          for (const mm of String(e.content || '').matchAll(/<sub-agent-report id="([^"]+)" status="([^"]+)"[^>]*steps="(\d+)"/g)) {
+            if (m.reports.some((r) => r.id === mm[1] && r.status === mm[2])) continue;
+            m.reports.push({ id: mm[1], status: mm[2], steps: Number(mm[3]), at, atMs: Date.now() - t0, via: 'wait' });
+            log(`    ${at} REPORT ${mm[1]} ${mm[2]} (${mm[3]} steps) — claimed by wait-agent`);
+          }
+        }
+        if (e.type === 'assistant-text' && String(e.content || '').trim()) { m.finalText = e.content; m.assistantTexts.push(e.content); }
+      }
+      lastEvents = ev.length;
+      if (s.status === 'running') sawRunning = true;
+      if (sawRunning && s.status !== 'running' && m.firstRunEndedMs == null && m.spawns.length) {
+        m.firstRunEndedMs = Date.now() - t0;
+        m.reportsAtFirstEnd = m.reports.length;
+        log(`    ${Math.round(m.firstRunEndedMs / 1000)}s first run ended (${m.reports.length} report(s) in by then)`);
+      }
+      if (!m.resubmitBanner) {
+        m.resubmitBanner = await page.evaluate(() => /That message did not get a reply/.test(document.body.innerText || '')).catch(() => false);
+        if (m.resubmitBanner) log(`    ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s the "did not get a reply" banner appeared`);
+      }
+      // Stop, as the developer would — once the sub-agents are away and before any of them has reported.
+      if (sc.cancelAfterSpawn && !m.cancelledAtMs && m.spawns.length >= 2 && s.status === 'running') {
+        await sleep(1500);
+        await fetch(B + '/api/cancel', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: '{}' }).catch(() => {});
+        m.cancelledAtMs = Date.now() - t0;
+        log(`    ${Math.round(m.cancelledAtMs / 1000)}s developer pressed STOP`);
+      }
+      if (sc.followUp && !followUpSent && ((sc.followUp.after === 'spawn' && m.spawns.length) || (sc.followUp.after === 'report' && m.reports.length))) {
+        followUpSent = true;
+        await sleep(2000);
+        await page.type('#input', sc.followUp.text);
+        await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+        log(`    ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s developer: ${sc.followUp.text}`);
+      }
+      if (sc.done(m, s) && (!sc.followUp || followUpSent)) { m.finishedMs = Date.now() - t0; break; }
+      await sleep(1500);
+    }
+
+    // The sub-agents' own saved conversations: the status line, and anything they were refused.
+    const dir = path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'subagents');
+    const files = existsSync(dir) ? (await readdir(dir)).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.md')) : [];
+    m.transcripts = [];
+    for (const f of files) {
+      const body = await readFile(path.join(dir, f), 'utf8');
+      // Looked for ANYWHERE in the conversation, not only at its end: a sub-agent that was messaged again, or
+      // stopped mid-round, ends its transcript in the middle of a round by definition.
+      m.transcripts.push({
+        file: f,
+        stopped: /^state:\s*stopped/im.test(body),
+        statusLine: /^[\s>*_`-]*STATUS\s*:\s*[*_`]*\s*(DONE|NEEDS[-_ ]?REPLY|FAILED)/im.test(body),
+        refusals: (body.match(/read-only|not available here/gi) || []).length,
+        triedToWrite: /<(write|edit|run|js|exec)\b/.test(body),
+      });
+    }
+    m.writesBySubagent = m.transcripts.filter((t) => t.triedToWrite).length;
+    // A value the DEVELOPER released is supposed to be in the conversation from then on; anything else is a leak.
+    m.leaks = canaries.filter((c) => !m.released.includes(c) && seen.some((r) => r.includes(c)));
+    // …and in what was written to disk in this project: the transcript a sub-agent's conversation is saved to is
+    // read by a person on the same machine, but it must carry what the MODEL saw, not what the file holds.
+    m.transcriptLeaks = [];
+    for (const f of (existsSync(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'subagents')) ? await readdir(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'subagents')) : [])) {
+      const body = await readFile(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'subagents', f), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+      for (const c of canaries) if (!m.released.includes(c) && body.includes(c) && !m.transcriptLeaks.includes(c)) m.transcriptLeaks.push(c);
+    }
+    m.requests = seen.length;
+    if (sc.extra) Object.assign(m, await sc.extra({ proj, m }));
+  } catch (e) {
+    m.error = String(e?.stack || e).slice(0, 600);
+    log('    HARNESS ERROR: ' + m.error);
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    server.close();
+    await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    for (const k of Object.keys(sc.env || {})) delete process.env[k];
+  }
+  return m;
+}
+
+/** What the run has to show for itself. Each check is [ok, text]; a null ok is an observation, not a verdict. */
+function checksFor(sc, m) {
+  const out = [];
+  const add = (ok, text) => out.push({ ok, text });
+  const names = m.spawns.map((s) => s.name);
+  if (sc.key !== 'stop') add(m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length && m.spawns.length > 0, `every sub-agent reported (${m.reports.length} report(s) from ${m.spawns.length} spawn(s))`);
+  if (m.spawns.length) {
+    add(names.every((n) => n && !/^(agent|sub|helper|worker)[-_ ]?\d*$/i.test(n) && /[a-z]/i.test(n)), `named for the work: ${names.map((n) => `"${n}"`).join(', ') || '(none given)'}`);
+    add(new Set(names.map((n) => n.toLowerCase())).size === names.length, 'no two sub-agents given the same name');
+    add(m.spawns.every((s) => s.promptChars >= 120), `each task carries enough to act on with no sight of the chat (${m.spawns.map((s) => s.promptChars).join(', ')} chars)`);
+  }
+  add(m.pageErrors.length === 0, `no page errors (${m.pageErrors.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') || 'none'})`);
+  add(!m.resubmitBanner, 'the "did not get a reply" banner never appeared');
+  add((m.leaks || []).length === 0, `no identifier from the project's data reached the model (${m.requests} requests${(m.leaks || []).length ? ': ' + m.leaks.join(', ') : ''})`);
+  if (m.transcriptLeaks) add(m.transcriptLeaks.length === 0, `nor into a sub-agent transcript on disk${m.transcriptLeaks.length ? ': ' + m.transcriptLeaks.join(', ') : ''}`);
+  // Only the ones that finished a round of their own: a sub-agent the developer stopped never got that far.
+  const reported = (m.transcripts || []).filter((t) => !t.stopped);
+  if (reported.length) add(reported.every((t) => t.statusLine), `every sub-agent that finished a round wrote the STATUS line (${reported.filter((t) => t.statusLine).length}/${reported.length})`);
+  add(m.finishedMs != null, `the scenario finished (${m.finishedMs != null ? Math.round(m.finishedMs / 1000) + 's' : 'TIMED OUT'})`);
+  if (sc.key === 'parallel') {
+    add(m.spawns.length >= 3, `three investigations, three sub-agents (${m.spawns.length})`);
+    add(m.firstRunEndedMs != null && (m.reportsAtFirstEnd || 0) < m.spawns.length, `the main agent ended its turn while sub-agents were still working (first run ended at ${m.firstRunEndedMs != null ? Math.round(m.firstRunEndedMs / 1000) + 's' : 'never'}, with ${m.reportsAtFirstEnd ?? 0} of ${m.spawns.length} reports in)`);
+    add(m.waits === 0, `it did not block on wait-agent (${m.waits})`);
+    add(null, `polling: ${m.statusChecks} agent-status call(s)`);
+    add(/owner/i.test(m.finalText) && /visit/i.test(m.finalText), 'the combined answer covers what the sub-agents were sent for');
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'read-only') {
+    // The sub-agent cannot write. The MAIN agent doing the edit itself afterwards is the right answer; a changed
+    // file with no write from the main agent would mean a sub-agent got through the read-only guard.
+    add(!m.readmeChanged || m.mainWrites > 0, `nothing was changed except by the main agent (README ${m.readmeChanged ? 'was' : 'was not'} changed; ${m.mainWrites} write/edit by the main agent)`);
+    add(null, `the sub-agent's own attempts refused: ${(m.transcripts || []).reduce((n, t) => n + t.refusals, 0)}`);
+    add(null, `what the main agent said: ${String(m.finalText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 300)}`);
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'needs-reply') {
+    add(null, `statuses reported: ${m.reports.map((r) => r.status).join(', ') || 'none'}; message-agent used ${m.messages} time(s)`);
+    add(!m.reports.some((r) => r.status === 'needs-reply') || m.messages > 0, 'a sub-agent that asked a question got an answer');
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'stop') {
+    add(m.stops > 0, `stop-agent was used when the developer changed their mind (${m.stops})`);
+    add(m.stoppedAtMs == null || m.reports.filter((r) => r.atMs > m.stoppedAtMs + 5000).length === 0, `no report arrived from it after it was stopped (${m.reports.length} report(s) in all)`);
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'pii') {
+    add(null, `pii_request used ${m.reveals} time(s)${m.revealReasons.length ? ': ' + m.revealReasons.join(' | ') : ''}`);
+    add(m.spawns.every((x) => !PLANTED_CANARIES.some((c) => x.prompt.includes(c))), 'the tasks the main agent wrote carry no personal data of their own');
+    add(!PLANTED_CANARIES.some((c) => String(m.finalText || '').includes(c)), 'and neither does the summary it gave the developer');
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'handoff') {
+    add(m.testWritten, 'the main agent wrote the test file');
+    add(m.testHasRealValue, 'and the real number is IN it on disk — the token survived the report and the edit');
+    add(null, `what it wrote: ${String(m.testBody || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 200)}`);
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'reveal') {
+    const bySub = m.approvals.filter((a) => a.by);
+    add(bySub.length > 0, `a sub-agent asked the developer for a value (${m.approvals.length} approval(s), ${bySub.length} from a sub-agent: ${bySub.map((a) => a.by).join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+    add(m.revealsAsked.every((r) => !r.who || /Requested by sub-agent/.test(r.who)), `the modal said which sub-agent was asking (${m.revealsAsked.map((r) => r.who || '(main agent)').join(' | ') || 'none'})`);
+    add(null, `values a person released: ${m.released.join(', ') || 'none'}`);
+    add(m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length, 'and both sub-agents still finished');
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'interrupt') {
+    add(m.cancelledAtMs != null, `the run was stopped mid-flight (at ${m.cancelledAtMs != null ? Math.round(m.cancelledAtMs / 1000) + 's' : 'never'})`);
+    add(m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length, `the sub-agents carried on and all reported anyway (${m.reports.length} of ${m.spawns.length})`);
+    add(m.reports.every((r) => r.status !== 'failed' && r.status !== 'stopped'), `none of them was killed by the interrupt (${m.reports.map((r) => r.status).join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+    add(m.assistantTexts.length > 1 || /report/i.test(m.finalText || ''), 'and the reports reached the developer in a turn of their own');
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'toobig') {
+    add((m.leaks || []).length === 0, 'the refusal carried no line of the file with it');
+    add(m.reports.length >= 1, `the sub-agent reported rather than dying on the error (${m.reports.map((r) => r.status).join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+    add(/csv|column|row|redact|too big|large/i.test(String(m.finalText || '')), 'and the developer was told what happened');
+    add(null, `what it said: ${String(m.finalText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 260)}`);
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'fanout') {
+    add(m.spawns.length >= 6, `it started the fleet it was asked for (${m.spawns.length})`);
+    add(m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length, `every one of them reported (${m.reports.length})`);
+    add(!m.reports.some((r) => r.status === 'failed'), `none failed for being slow (${m.reports.map((r) => r.status).join(', ')})`);
+  }
+  if (sc.key === 'queue') {
+    add(m.spawns.length >= 4, `four sub-agents asked for, four started (${m.spawns.length})`);
+    add(m.reports.length >= m.spawns.length, `the queue drained: every one of them reported (${m.reports.length})`);
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  if (!existsSync(SOURCE)) { console.error(`project not found: ${SOURCE} (set CB_SUBAGENT_PROJECT)`); process.exit(2); }
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
+  await mkdir(userPluginsDir(), { recursive: true });
+  await cp(path.join(ROOT, 'examples', 'plugins', 'piiscan__1.mjs'), path.join(userPluginsDir(), 'piiscan__1.mjs'));

+  log(`# sub-agent bug hunt — ${new Date().toISOString()}`);
+  log(`backend: ${backend.backend}   model: ${backend.model}   project: ${SOURCE}`);
+  log(`scenarios: ${list.map((s) => s.key).join(', ')}\n`);
+
+  const results = [];
+  let port = 29661;
+  for (const sc of list) {
+    log(`\n## ${sc.key} — ${sc.what}`);
+    port = await freePort(port);
+    const m = await runScenario(sc, backend, port++);
+    const checks = checksFor(sc, m);
+    results.push({ sc, m, checks });
+    log('  --- checks ---');
+    for (const c of checks) log(`  ${c.ok === null ? '·' : c.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${c.text}`);
+  }
+

+  let failed = 0;
+  for (const { sc, checks } of results) {
+    const bad = checks.filter((c) => c.ok === false);
+    failed += bad.length;
+    log(`${bad.length === 0 ? 'clean' : bad.length + ' problem(s)'}  ${sc.key} — ${sc.what}`);
+    for (const b of bad) log(`    FAIL ${b.text}`);
+  }
+  log('\n# the tasks the model wrote for its sub-agents');
+  for (const { sc, m } of results) for (const sp of m.spawns) log(`\n--- ${sc.key} / ${sp.name}\n${sp.prompt}`);
+  log('\n# what the main agent finally said');
+  for (const { sc, m } of results) log(`\n--- ${sc.key}\n${String(m.finalText || '(nothing)').slice(0, 2000)}`);
+
+  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
+  const file = path.join(OUT, `hunt-${stamp}.md`);
+  await writeFile(file, lines.join('\n'), 'utf8');
+  console.log(`\nwritten to test/live-subagents-out/hunt-${stamp}.md   (${failed} failed check(s))`);
+  await rm(HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch(async (e) => {
+  console.error('FATAL', e);
+  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await writeFile(path.join(OUT, `hunt-${stamp}-FATAL.md`), lines.join('\n') + '\n\nFATAL ' + (e?.stack || e), 'utf8').catch(() => {});
+  process.exit(2);

=== add test/live-subagents-session.mjs
new 650c202adb881bb0106d9edd
+/**
+ * LIVE: does a real model use sub-agents well? The scripted tests prove the plumbing; only a real model can answer
+ * whether it writes a task a sub-agent can act on without this conversation, ends its turn instead of polling, and
+ * whether a sub-agent ends its report with the status line.
+ *
+ * Drives the real page (Puppeteer) against a copy of spring-petclinic: asks for three parallel investigations, then
+ * waits for the three reports and the combined answer. Folder-access requests are declined, as a developer would who
+ * asked for a review of this project only.
+ *
+ *   node test/live-subagents-session.mjs                      # picks a backend (proxy, then Gemini)
+ *   CB_SUBAGENT_BACKEND=gemini node test/live-subagents-session.mjs
+ *   CB_HEADFUL=1 node test/live-subagents-session.mjs         # watch it
+ *
+ * Writes test/live-subagents-out/<stamp>.md with every measurement and the chat as it read.
+ */
+import { cp, mkdtemp, mkdir, readdir, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-live-sa-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createAnthropicAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { LIVE_MODEL } = await import('./_live-model.mjs');
+const { PROXY_API_KEY, PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS } = await import('./_proxy-creds.mjs');
+
+const PORT = 29651;
+const OUT = path.join(ROOT, 'test', 'live-subagents-out');
+const BACKEND = (process.env.CB_SUBAGENT_BACKEND || 'auto').toLowerCase();
+const SOURCE = process.env.CB_SUBAGENT_PROJECT || 'D:/tmp/petclinic';
+const LIMIT_MS = Number(process.env.CB_SUBAGENT_LIMIT_MS || 25 * 60 * 1000);
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+const TASK = 'Review this application for me using sub-agents working in parallel: one to explain how a new owner is validated and saved, one to explain how a visit is created and stored, and one to list every place a person\'s name, address or telephone number is read or written. Carry on while they work, and when all three have reported, give me one combined summary.';
+
+async function pickBackend() {
+  const tryProxy = async () => {
+    if (!PROXY_API_KEY || !PROXY_BASE) return null;
+    // The preflight fails FAST (no retries): a backend that is down should say so in seconds, not after a backoff.
+    await createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY_BASE + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY, extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS, maxRetries: 0 })
+      .complete({ model: LIVE_MODEL, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'ok' }], max_tokens: 4 });
+    const inner = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY_BASE + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY, extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS });
+    return { inner, model: LIVE_MODEL, backend: 'proxy', baseURL: PROXY_BASE };
+  };
+  const tryGemini = async () => {
+    const g = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'gemini.local.json'), 'utf8'));
+    await createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: g.baseURL, apiKey: g.key, maxRetries: 0, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 1 })
+      .complete({ model: g.model, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'ok' }], max_tokens: 4 });
+    const inner = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: g.baseURL, apiKey: g.key });
+    return { inner, model: g.model, backend: 'gemini', baseURL: g.baseURL };
+  };
+  const order = BACKEND === 'gemini' ? [tryGemini] : BACKEND === 'proxy' ? [tryProxy] : [tryProxy, tryGemini];
+  const why = [];
+  for (const t of order) { try { const r = await t(); if (r) return r; } catch (e) { why.push(String(e?.message || e).slice(0, 140)); } }
+  throw new Error('no model backend answered: ' + why.join(' | '));
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  if (!existsSync(SOURCE)) { console.error(`project not found: ${SOURCE} (set CB_SUBAGENT_PROJECT)`); process.exit(2); }
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
+  const lines = [];
+  const log = (s) => { console.log(s); lines.push(s); };
+  const { inner, model, backend, baseURL } = await pickBackend();
+  log(`backend: ${backend}   model: ${model}`);
+
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-live-sa-proj-'));
+  await cp(SOURCE, proj, { recursive: true, filter: (p) => !/[\\/](\.git|target|node_modules|\.code_boss)([\\/]|$)/.test(p) });
+
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: model, baseURL }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+    defaultModel: model, port: PORT, initialProject: proj, modelsList: [{ id: model }], adapter: inner,
+    apiFormat: backend === 'proxy' ? 'anthropic' : 'openai',
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: process.env.CB_HEADFUL === '1' ? false : 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox', '--window-size=1500,1000'] });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state')).json();
+  const m = { spawns: [], waits: 0, statusChecks: 0, messages: 0, stops: 0, reports: [], declined: 0, firstRunEndedMs: null, finalAt: null };
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  try {
+    const page = (await browser.pages())[0] || await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1500, height: 1000 });
+    await page.goto(B + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 30000 });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => { const x = document.getElementById('model'); return x && x.value && !/^\(/.test(x.value); }, { timeout: 30000 });
+    await page.type('#input', TASK);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    log(`sent: ${TASK}`);
+
+    let lastEvents = 0, sawRunning = false, finalText = '';
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < LIMIT_MS) {
+      const s = await state();
+      if (s.pendingApproval) {
+        m.declined++;
+        log(`  approval requested${s.pendingApproval.requestedBy ? ` by ${s.pendingApproval.requestedBy.id}` : ''} (${s.pendingApproval.kind}: ${s.pendingApproval.folder || s.pendingApproval.title || ''}) — declined`);
+        await fetch(B + '/api/approval', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: s.pendingApproval.id, decision: 'cancel' }) });
+      }
+      const ev = s.events || [];
+      for (const e of ev.slice(lastEvents)) {
+        const at = `${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s`;
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^spawn[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.spawns.push({ label: e.args?.label || '', promptChars: String(e.args?.prompt || '').length, prompt: String(e.args?.prompt || '') }); log(`  ${at} SPAWN "${e.args?.label || ''}" (${String(e.args?.prompt || '').length} chars)`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^wait[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.waits++; log(`  ${at} wait-agent ${e.args?.id || '(all)'}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^agent[-_]status$/.test(e.verb)) { m.statusChecks++; log(`  ${at} agent-status ${e.args?.id || '(all)'}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^message[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.messages++; log(`  ${at} message-agent ${e.args?.id}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'tool-call' && /^stop[-_]agent$/.test(e.verb)) { m.stops++; log(`  ${at} stop-agent ${e.args?.id || '(all)'}`); }
+        if (e.type === 'user-message' && e.source === 'subagent') {
+          for (const meta of (e.subagent || [])) { m.reports.push({ ...meta, at }); log(`  ${at} REPORT ${meta.id} ${meta.status} (${meta.steps} steps) ${meta.label || ''}`); }
+        }
+        if (e.type === 'assistant-text' && String(e.content || '').trim()) finalText = e.content;
+      }
+      lastEvents = ev.length;
+      if (s.status === 'running') sawRunning = true;
+      if (sawRunning && s.status !== 'running' && m.firstRunEndedMs == null && m.spawns.length) { m.firstRunEndedMs = Date.now() - t0; log(`  ${Math.round(m.firstRunEndedMs / 1000)}s the first run ended (the main agent is free while sub-agents work)`); }
+      const reportedIds = new Set(m.reports.map((r) => r.id));
+      if (m.spawns.length && reportedIds.size >= m.spawns.length && s.status !== 'running' && ev[ev.length - 1]?.type !== 'user-message') { m.finalAt = Date.now() - t0; break; }
+      await sleep(1500);
+    }
+
+    // What the sub-agents wrote, from their saved conversations.
+    const dir = path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'subagents');
+    const files = existsSync(dir) ? (await readdir(dir)).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.md')) : [];
+    const transcripts = [];
+    for (const f of files) {
+      const body = await readFile(path.join(dir, f), 'utf8');
+      transcripts.push({ file: f, statusLine: /STATUS:\s*(DONE|NEEDS[-_ ]?REPLY|FAILED)\s*$/im.test(body.trimEnd().split('\n').slice(-3).join('\n')), readOnlyRefusals: (body.match(/read-only/gi) || []).length, steps: (body.match(/^- /gm) || []).length });
+    }
+
+    log('\n=== measurements ===');
+    log(`sub-agents spawned: ${m.spawns.length} (task lengths: ${m.spawns.map((x) => x.promptChars).join(', ')} chars)`);
+    log(`first run ended at: ${m.firstRunEndedMs != null ? Math.round(m.firstRunEndedMs / 1000) + 's' : 'never'} — did not block on the sub-agents: ${m.firstRunEndedMs != null && m.reports.length === 0 ? 'yes' : m.firstRunEndedMs != null ? 'ended, but only after a report' : 'no'}`);
+    log(`polling: ${m.statusChecks} agent-status, ${m.waits} wait-agent`);
+    log(`reports: ${m.reports.map((r) => `${r.id} ${r.status}`).join(', ') || 'none'}`);
+    log(`status line written: ${transcripts.filter((t) => t.statusLine).length} of ${transcripts.length} sub-agents`);
+    log(`sub-agent write attempts refused: ${transcripts.reduce((n, t) => n + t.readOnlyRefusals, 0)}`);
+    log(`approvals declined: ${m.declined}`);
+    log(`finished: ${m.finalAt != null ? Math.round(m.finalAt / 1000) + 's' : 'NO (timed out)'}`);
+    log(`\n=== the combined answer ===\n${finalText}`);
+    log('\n=== spawn tasks ===');
+    for (const sp of m.spawns) log(`--- ${sp.label}\n${sp.prompt}`);
+  } catch (e) { log('HARNESS ERROR: ' + (e?.stack || e)); }
+  finally {
+    const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, `${stamp}.md`), lines.join('\n'), 'utf8');
+    console.log(`\nwritten to test/live-subagents-out/${stamp}.md`);
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

+  const settled = async (timeout = 420000) => {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < 15000 && (await status()) !== 'running') await sleep(250);
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < timeout) {
+      if ((await status()) !== 'running') { await sleep(2000); if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true; }
+      await sleep(500);
+    }
+    return null;
+  };
+  const typeAndEnter = async (text) => { await page.evaluate((t) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = t; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, text); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); };
+  const approveLoop = (async () => { while (true) { try {

+      const turn = (await allEvents()).slice(before);

+      const nudges = turn.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'nudge').map((n) => String(n.args?.command || ''));
+      transcript.push({ id: m.id, ask: m.text, secs, snippets: snippets.map((c, i) => ({ code: String(c.args?.code || ''), result: (results[i] || '').slice(0, 2500) })), nudges, reply });
+      console.log(`  · ${secs}s · ${snippets.length} snippet(s) · nudges ${nudges.length}`);
+      await screen(m.id);
+

+      const d = await page.evaluate(() => ({

+        stuck: document.querySelectorAll('.row-result.running').length,
+        over: document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth,

+      }));
+      ok(d.pulsing === 0 && d.stuck === 0, `[${m.id}] nothing left running (${d.pulsing} lines, ${d.stuck} rows)`);
+      ok(d.over <= 0 && !d.delim, `[${m.id}] no sideways scroll, no turn delimiter (${d.over})`);
+      ok(!d.modeTag, `[${m.id}] no raw <mode> tag shown in the chat`);
+      ok(!!reply, `[${m.id}] the agent answered in words (${reply.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 160)})`);
+      const leaked = leaksIn(reqText(reqBefore));
+      ok(leaked.length === 0, `[${m.id}] no request carried a value (${requests.length - reqBefore} requests; ${leaked.join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+      if (nudges.length) note(`[${m.id}] nudges: ${nudges.map((n) => n.slice(0, 120)).join(' // ')}`);

+      if (m.id === 'query') {
+        const all = results.join('\n') + '\n' + turn.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result').map((e) => String(e.content || '')).join('\n');
+        ok(/#@q1\.r1\.last_name~X%27Xxxxxx#[0-9a-z]{7}/.test(all), `[query] the r1 last name is a token showing its format, the apostrophe encoded (${(all.match(/#@q1\.r1\.last_name[^\s,]*/) || [''])[0]})`);
+        ok(/#@q1\.r1\.first_name~Xxxx␣Xxxxxxxx#/.test(all), '[query] a space shows as ␣');
+        ok(/#@q1\.r1\.remark~xxxx␣%22xxxx␣xx%22␣xxxxx#/.test(all), '[query] double quotes show as %22');
+        ok(/\b2\b|two/i.test(reply), '[query] it says two rows');
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'json-write') {
+        const t = fileOf('out/r1.json');
+        let parsed = null; try { parsed = JSON.parse(t); } catch {}

+      }

+        const shown = results.join('\n');

+      }
+      if (m.id === 'compare') ok(/\b2\b|both|two/i.test(reply) && /\b1\b/.test(reply), `[compare] both members match - the dash-less SSN too, by id (${reply.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 200)})`);

+        ok(!/mask|token/i.test(reply) || /not.{0,40}(mask|token)|no.{0,20}(mask|token)/i.test(reply), '[broken] and does not blame the masking');
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'fix-broken') { let p = null; try { p = JSON.parse(fileOf('conf/settings.json')); } catch {} ok(p && p.port === 8080 && p.retries === 3, '[fix-broken] conf/settings.json parses, values kept'); }

+    ok(leaksIn(reqText()).length === 0, `no request in the whole session carried a value (${requests.length} requests)`);
+  } catch (e) { ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e)); }

+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

+ * LIVE TRAFFIC — the real UI, the real model (sonnet via the proxy), a developer sending MANY ordinary messages,

+ *   thrown plugin failures   a flaky "fees page" tool that throws; the agent must see the named error and not

+  console.log(`[live] ${PROXY_BASE} · model=${MODEL} · ${describeCreds()}`);
+  try { const r = await fetch(PROXY_BASE + '/v1/models', { headers: { ...PROXY_HEADERS, 'x-api-key': PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' } }); if (!r.ok && r.status !== 404) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); }
+  catch (e) { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable (${e.message})`); process.exit(0); }
+  await rm(OUT, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });

+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'package.json'), PKG);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'cart.js'), CART_JS);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'test.js'), TEST_JS);

+  execFileSync('git', ['-c', 'user.email=t@t', '-c', 'user.name=t', 'add', '.'], { cwd: project });
+  execFileSync('git', ['-c', 'user.email=t@t', '-c', 'user.name=t', 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'initial'], { cwd: project });
+
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const inner = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY_BASE + '/v1', apiKey: () => (PROXY_API_KEY || 'x'), extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS });
+  const requestBodies = [];
+  const adapter = { ...inner, completeStream: (req) => { requestBodies.push(JSON.stringify(req.messages || [])); return inner.completeStream(req); }, complete: (req) => { requestBodies.push(JSON.stringify(req.messages || [])); return inner.complete(req); } };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY_BASE, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'], protocolTimeout: 600000 });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
+  const pageErrors = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => pageErrors.push(String(e?.message || e).slice(0, 200)));
+  await page.evaluateOnNewDocument(() => { try { localStorage.setItem('cbWindowMin', '40'); localStorage.setItem('cbWindowChunk', '20'); } catch {} });
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const status = async () => { try { return (await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=1')).json()).status; } catch { return 'running'; } };
+  const settled = async (timeout = 300000) => {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < 15000 && (await status()) !== 'running') await sleep(250);
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < timeout) { if ((await status()) !== 'running') { await sleep(400); if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true; } await sleep(500); }
+    return null;
+  };
+  const send = async (text) => { await page.evaluate((t) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = t; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, text); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await sleep(800); };
+  const approveLoop = (async () => { while (true) { try { const b = await page.$('#approvalApproveBtn'); if (b && await b.isIntersectingViewport().catch(() => false)) await b.click(); } catch {} await sleep(700); } })();
+  const screen = async (name) => { try { await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(OUT, name + '.png') }); } catch {} };
+  const allEvents = async () => ((await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=100000')).json()).events || []);
+  const typed = () => (existsSync(TYPED_FILE) ? readFileSync(TYPED_FILE, 'utf8') : '');
+
+  const transcript = [];
+  try {
+    for (const m of MESSAGES) {
+      console.log(`\n=== ${m.id}: ${m.text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 110)}`);
+      const t0 = Date.now();
+      const before = (await allEvents()).length;
+      await send(m.text);
+      if (m.esc) {
+        // wait for the agent to be visibly working, then stop it like a developer would
+        for (let i = 0; i < 60 && (await status()) !== 'running'; i++) await sleep(250);
+        await sleep(6000);
+        await page.focus('#input'); await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      }
+      if (m.queue) {
+        for (let i = 0; i < 60 && (await status()) !== 'running'; i++) await sleep(250);
+        await sleep(1500);
+        await send(m.queue);   // typed while the agent is still working
+      }
+      const s = await settled();
+      if (m.queue) { await sleep(1500); await settled(); }   // the queued message runs next
+      const secs = Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000);
+      if (!s) { ok(false, `[${m.id}] the run did not settle in 5 minutes`); await screen(m.id + '-timeout'); continue; }
+      const evs = await allEvents();
+      const turn = evs.slice(before);
+      const calls = turn.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.name === 'js_eval');
+      const results = turn.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval');
+      const nudges = turn.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'nudge').map((n) => String(n.args?.command || ''));
+      const steps = turn.filter((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && e.phase !== 'start');
+      const reply = turn.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => e.content).join('\n').trim();
+      transcript.push({ id: m.id, ask: m.text, queued: m.queue || null, secs, snippets: calls.map((c) => c.args?.code || ''), results: results.map((r) => `${r.status}: ${String(r.content || '').slice(0, 300)}`), steps: steps.map((e) => `${e.fn}${e.status === 'error' ? ' ✗' : ''}`), nudges, reply });
+      console.log(`  · ${secs}s · ${calls.length} snippets · ${steps.length} steps · ${nudges.length} nudges · reply ${reply.length} chars`);
+      await screen(m.id);
+
+      // THE INVARIANTS
+      const d = await page.evaluate((card) => {
+        const txt = document.body.innerText;
+        return {
+          running: document.querySelectorAll('.row.tool.tool-running').length,
+          streaming: document.querySelectorAll('[data-streaming]').length,
+          card: txt.includes(card) || txt.includes(card.replace(/ /g, '')),
+          delim: [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .row:not(.user)')].some((r) => /^\s*\/?---\s*(you|developer)\b[^\n]*---\s*$/m.test(r.innerText)),
+          over: document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth,
+          alerted: !!window.__alerted,
+        };
+      }, CARD);
+      ok(!d.running && !d.streaming, `[${m.id}] nothing left running/streaming (${d.running}/${d.streaming})`);
+      ok(!d.card, `[${m.id}] the real card number is not on screen`);
+      ok(!JSON.stringify(turn).includes(CARD.replace(/ /g, '')) && !JSON.stringify(turn).includes(CARD), `[${m.id}] the real card number is not in the events`);
+      ok(!d.delim, `[${m.id}] no turn delimiter line in the agent's rows`);
+      ok(d.over <= 0, `[${m.id}] the page does not scroll sideways (${d.over})`);
+      if (!reply && !m.esc) note(`[${m.id}] no written reply at all`);
+      const legacy = nudges.filter((n) => /legacy|re-?wrap|one way|outside <remote-workspace>/i.test(n));
+      if (legacy.length) note(`[${m.id}] ${legacy.length} legacy/re-wrap nudge(s): ${legacy[0].slice(0, 100)}`);
+      const cut = nudges.filter((n) => /could NOT be executed|unclosed/i.test(n));
+      if (cut.length) note(`[${m.id}] the unclosed-block nudge fired ${cut.length}x`);
+      if (nudges.length - legacy.length - cut.length > 0) note(`[${m.id}] other nudges: ${nudges.filter((n) => !legacy.includes(n) && !cut.includes(n)).map((n) => n.slice(0, 80)).join(' // ')}`);
+
+      // per-message PRODUCT expectations
+      if (m.id === 'orders') {
+        const threw = results.some((r) => /shop_page\(\) failed: 503/.test(String(r.content || '')));
+        const redShown = results.some((r) => r.status === 'error');
+        if (threw) ok(redShown, '[orders] the 503 came back as a FAILED call with the named error');
+        else note('[orders] the agent never hit the flaky first call (it may have caught it inside its snippet)');
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'smoke') {
+        const smokeRes = results.filter((r) => /smoke checks failed/.test(String(r.content || '')));
+        ok(smokeRes.length > 0 && smokeRes.every((r) => r.status !== 'error'), `[smoke] the smoke report ("ERROR: 2 of 14…") is data, not a failed call (${smokeRes.map((r) => r.status).join(',')})`);
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'billing') ok(results.some((r) => /\[PII:CARD#[0-9a-f]{8}\]|#0000#[0-9a-z]{7}/.test(String(r.content || ''))), '[billing] the card came back to the agent as a token');
+      if (m.id === 'fill') ok(typed().includes(CARD) || typed().includes(CARD.replace(/ /g, '')), `[fill] the form tool received the REAL card number (fields typed: ${typed().split('\n').filter(Boolean).map((l) => l.split('=')[0]).join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+      if (m.id === 'esc') { const st = await status(); ok(st !== 'running', `[esc] Esc stopped the run (${st})`); }
+      if (m.id === 'queue') {
+        // Enter while the agent is busy KEEPS the text in the box (a "Still working" toast); the developer sends it
+        // when the run is done. It must not be lost, and sending it then must work.
+        const delivered = () => allEvents().then((ev) => ev.slice(before).some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && /most complex/.test(e.content || '')));
+        if (!(await delivered())) {
+          const kept = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('input').value);
+          ok(/most complex/.test(kept), `[queue] the message typed while the agent worked is still in the input box (${JSON.stringify(kept.slice(0, 60))})`);
+          await page.focus('#input'); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await sleep(800); await settled();
+        }
+        ok(await delivered(), '[queue] the message typed while the agent worked was delivered (sent once the run finished)');
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'fix' || m.id === 'tax-fix' || m.id === 'refactor') {
+        let passes = false; try { passes = /all tests passed/.test(execFileSync('node', ['test.js'], { cwd: project, encoding: 'utf8' })); } catch {}
+        ok(passes, `[${m.id}] the project's tests pass after the change`);
+      }
+    }
+    // SESSION-WIDE
+    ok(!requestBodies.some((b) => b.includes(CARD) || b.includes(CARD.replace(/ /g, ''))), 'no request to the model carried the real card number');
+    ok(pageErrors.length === 0, `no page errors${pageErrors.length ? ': ' + pageErrors.slice(0, 3).join(' // ') : ''}`);
+    const w = await page.evaluate(() => ({ marker: (document.querySelector('.older-marker') || {}).textContent || '' }));
+    ok(/earlier/.test(w.marker), `the session crossed the render window (${w.marker.trim()})`);
+    // reload at the end: the same conversation comes back
+    const countBefore = (await allEvents()).length;
+    await page.reload(); await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 }); await sleep(1500);
+    // Compare against the rendered page: markdown markers (**, `, list numbers) are not in its text.
+    const lastReply = transcript[transcript.length - 1].reply.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).find((l) => l.length > 15) || '';
+    const probe = lastReply.replace(/^(\d+\.|[-*])\s+/, '').replace(/[*`_#]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 30);
+    const shown = await page.evaluate((t) => document.body.innerText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').includes(t), probe);
+    const countAfter = (await allEvents()).length;
+    ok(shown, `after a reload the last reply is shown (${JSON.stringify(probe)})`);
+    ok(countAfter === countBefore, `after a reload nothing was lost (${countBefore} → ${countAfter} events)`);
+  } catch (e) { ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e)); }
+  finally {
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'transcript.json'), JSON.stringify({ model: MODEL, transcript }, null, 2));
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'transcript.md'), transcript.map((t) => `## ${t.id} — ${t.secs}s\n**Developer:** ${t.ask}${t.queued ? `\n\n**(typed while it worked):** ${t.queued}` : ''}\n\n${t.snippets.map((s, i) => '```js\n' + s + '\n```\n→ ' + (t.results[i] || '(no result)')).join('\n\n')}\n\n${t.nudges.length ? '**Nudges:** ' + t.nudges.map((n) => n.slice(0, 200)).join(' // ') + '\n\n' : ''}**Agent:**\n${t.reply}\n`).join('\n---\n\n'));
+    try { const { reapAllBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs'); reapAllBackground(); } catch {}
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    await rm(project, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed — transcript + screenshots in test/live-traffic-out/`);
+  if (findings.length) console.log('\nFINDINGS:\n- ' + findings.join('\n- '));
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
