+/**
+ * LIVE VOLUME + BUG HUNT (build 1965) — the real UI, the real model (sonnet via the proxy), a developer doing a
+ * normal session's worth of work, with the awkward moments real sessions have mixed in: typing while it works
+ * (Enter steers), Esc mid-run, a reload mid-run, ⇱ on a slow step, a background run, a failing command, a big output,
+ * unicode, a very long message, "?". The LLM activity tab is ON the whole time.
+ *
+ * After EVERY message, the product:
+ *   - the run settles; nothing pulses that is not a live background snippet; no stuck "Running…" rows
+ *   - no turn delimiter in the agent's rows; no sideways scroll; no page errors
+ *   - LLM activity: one lane per model call the server recorded; no lane left live once idle; a green segment's
+ *     popup shows text

+ * snippet sizes/modes, nudges.

+ */

+function tax(amount, rate) { return Math.round(amount * rate * 100) / 100; }
+function discount(amount, pct) { return amount - Math.floor(amount * pct) / 100; }   // TODO: pct handling looks off
+function total(items, rate, pct) { const s = discount(subtotal(items), pct || 0); return s + tax(s, rate); }

+    const t0 = Date.now();

+    while (Date.now() - t0 < timeout) {

+        await sleep(1500);
+        if ((await status()) === 'running') continue;
+        if (drainBackground && (await bgSnippetsAlive()).length) { await sleep(1000); continue; }
+        await sleep(1500);
+        if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true;
+      }
+      await sleep(500);
+    }
+    return null;

+  const screen = async (name) => { try { await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(OUT, name + '.png') }); } catch {} };

+  const llmCalls = async () => ((await (await fetch(B + '/api/llm-activity')).json()).calls || []);

+      await typeAndEnter(m.text);

+      if (m.steer) { await waitRunning(); await sleep(m.steer.after); if ((await status()) === 'running') { await typeAndEnter(m.steer.text); note(`[${m.id}] typed while it worked (Enter steers): "${m.steer.text}"`); } else note(`[${m.id}] it finished before the steer could be typed`); }
+      if (m.esc) { await waitRunning(); await sleep(m.esc); await page.focus('#input'); await page.keyboard.press('Escape'); }
+      if (m.reload) { await waitRunning(); await sleep(m.reload); await page.reload(); await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 }); await page.evaluate(() => selectSideTab('llm')); }
+      let pressed = false;

+      const s = await settled(300000, { drainBackground: !nextIsMeanwhile });
+      const secs = Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000);
+      if (!s) { ok(false, `[${m.id}] did not settle in 5 minutes`); await screen(m.id + '-timeout'); continue; }

+      }

+// LIVE: the session transport with a REAL model behind the stand-in gateway (the developer, 2026-10-13: "build a simple
+// service that behaves similarly to the prod api and calls sonnet"). The real server, the real adapter stack, the
+// gateway of test/_gateway-sim.mjs, and Sonnet (CB_MODEL to change it) through the development proxy.
+//
+// The same three messages are run twice in two copies of one small project: with the transport OFF (the whole
+// conversation every step - the app as it is) and ON. What is compared is the OUTCOME - the bug fixed, the tests
+// passing when run from here, a question answered from the first task - and what went over the wire.
+//
+// Not part of run-all (no "test-" prefix): it needs the proxy. Run: node test/live-wire-session.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-wirel-home-'));
+delete process.env.CB_SESSION_TRANSPORT;
+// more follow-ups than the default before a fresh send, so the last question is answered from what the SESSION holds
+process.env.CB_WIRE_MAX_FOLLOWUPS = '12';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { startGatewaySim, liveBackend } = await import('./_gateway-sim.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PLAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview';
+
+const backend = await liveBackend();
+if (!backend || !(await backend.health())) { console.log('  SKIP - the development proxy is not reachable (proxy.local.json)'); process.exit(0); }
+console.log(`model behind the gateway: ${backend.model}`);
+
+const MESSAGES = [
+  'The test in test/price.test.mjs fails. Find out why, fix the bug in src/price.mjs, and run the test with node to confirm it passes.',
+  'Now add a function average(items) to src/price.mjs that returns the mean price of the items (0 for an empty list), add a test for it to test/price.test.mjs, and run the tests.',
+  'Looking back at the first thing I asked: in one sentence, what exactly was wrong in the code?',
+];
+const seed = async (proj) => {
+  await mkdir(path.join(proj, 'src'), { recursive: true }); await mkdir(path.join(proj, 'test'), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'pricing', type: 'module', private: true }, null, 2) + '\n');
+  // the bug: the discount is added instead of taken off
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'price.mjs'), 'export function total(items, discountPct = 0) {\n  const sum = items.reduce((a, i) => a + i.price * i.qty, 0);\n  return Math.round(sum * (1 + discountPct / 100) * 100) / 100;\n}\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'test', 'price.test.mjs'), "import assert from 'node:assert';\nimport { total } from '../src/price.mjs';\n\nassert.strictEqual(total([{ price: 10, qty: 2 }, { price: 5, qty: 1 }]), 25);\nassert.strictEqual(total([{ price: 100, qty: 1 }], 10), 90);\nconsole.log('price tests passed');\n");
+};
+
+async function scenario(label, port, transportOn) {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), `cb-wirel-${label}-`));
+  await seed(proj);
+  const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend });
+  const adapter = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', maxRetries: 2 });
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<!doctype html><body>x</body>', baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: PLAIN, port, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: PLAIN }], initialProject: proj, apiFormat: 'openai' });
+  const BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
+  const post = async (p, body) => (await fetch(BASE + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })).json().catch(() => null);
+  const state = async () => (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state?limit=2000')).json();
+  if (transportOn) await post('/api/agent-flags', { flag: 'sessionTransport', on: true });
+  const out = { label, proj, replies: [], perMessage: [], ms: [], gw };
+  try {
+    for (const text of MESSAGES) {
+      const before = (await state()).events?.length ?? 0, n = gw.log.length, t0 = Date.now();
+      await post('/api/message', { text });
+      let done = false;
+      for (let i = 0; i < 1500 && !done; i++) {   // up to ~10 minutes a message
+        await sleep(400);
+        const s = await state(); const evs = s.events || [];
+        if (evs.length > before && evs.slice(before).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text') && (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done')) { await sleep(600); const s2 = await state(); if (s2.status === 'idle' || s2.status === 'done') { done = true; out.replies.push((s2.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => e.content).pop() || ''); } }
+      }
+      if (!done) throw new Error(`[${label}] the run did not finish: ${text.slice(0, 50)}`);
+      out.ms.push(Date.now() - t0);
+      out.perMessage.push(gw.log.slice(n));
+      console.log(`  [${label}] message ${out.perMessage.length}: ${gw.log.slice(n).map((e) => (e.status !== 200 ? 'E' + e.status : e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W') + Math.round(e.readChars / 1000) + 'k').join(' ')}  (${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s)`);
+    }
+    out.events = (await state()).events || [];
+    out.testRun = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['test/price.test.mjs'], { cwd: proj, encoding: 'utf8' });
+    out.src = await readFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'price.mjs'), 'utf8');
+    out.testSrc = await readFile(path.join(proj, 'test', 'price.test.mjs'), 'utf8');
+  } finally { await new Promise((r) => { try { server.closeAllConnections?.(); } catch {} server.close(() => r()); }); await gw.close(); }
+  return out;
+}
+
+const judge = (o) => {
+  const all = o.perMessage.flat();
+  ok(o.testRun.status === 0 && /price tests passed/.test(o.testRun.stdout), `[${o.label}] the project's test passes when run from here (exit ${o.testRun.status})`);
+  ok(/1 - discountPct|100 - discountPct|- sum \*|-\s*discount/.test(o.src) && !/1 \+ discountPct/.test(o.src), `[${o.label}] the bug is fixed in src/price.mjs: the discount is taken off`);
+  ok(/export function average/.test(o.src) && /average/.test(o.testSrc), `[${o.label}] average() was added, with a test`);
+  ok(/discount/i.test(o.replies[2] || '') && /(add|plus|\+|increas|instead of (subtract|reduc|taking))/i.test(o.replies[2] || ''), `[${o.label}] asked about the first task two messages later, it names the bug (${String(o.replies[2]).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 110)})`);
+  ok(all.every((e) => e.status === 200), `[${o.label}] no request failed at the gateway`);
+  return { requests: all.length, whole: all.filter((e) => !e.sentSession).length, onSession: all.filter((e) => e.sentSession).length, chars: all.reduce((a, e) => a + e.readChars, 0), seconds: Math.round(o.ms.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 1000) };
+};
+
+let off = null, on = null;
+try {
+  console.log('\n[off] the whole conversation every step');
+  off = await scenario('off', 29491, false);
+  console.log('\n[on] the session transport');
+  on = await scenario('on', 29492, true);
+  console.log('');
+  const a = judge(off), b = judge(on);
+  ok(a.onSession === 0, `[off] nothing went on a session (${a.requests} requests, all whole)`);
+  ok(b.onSession >= 3 && b.whole >= 1 && b.whole < b.requests, `[on] most steps went on the session (${b.onSession} on the session, ${b.whole} whole, of ${b.requests})`);
+  ok(b.chars < a.chars * 0.6, `[on] far less was sent: ${Math.round(b.chars / 1000)}k characters against ${Math.round(a.chars / 1000)}k with it off`);
+  const retries = (on.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'retry' && /session/.test(JSON.stringify(e.args || {}))).length;
+  ok(retries === 0, `[on] the session was never lost (${retries} retries)`);
+  const first = on.perMessage.map((m) => (m[0].sentSession ? 'session' : 'whole'));
+  ok(first[0] === 'whole' && first[1] === 'session' && first[2] === 'session', `[on] the second and third messages continued the session the first one opened (${first.join(', ')})`);
+  ok(on.perMessage[2].length === 1 && on.perMessage[2][0].readChars < 3000, `[on] so the question about the first task was ONE small message, answered from what the session kept (${on.perMessage[2][0].readChars} chars)`);
+  console.log(`\n  off: ${a.requests} requests, ${Math.round(a.chars / 1000)}k chars sent, ${a.seconds}s`);
+  console.log(`  on:  ${b.requests} requests (${b.whole} whole, ${b.onSession} on the session), ${Math.round(b.chars / 1000)}k chars sent, ${b.seconds}s`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { for (const o of [off, on]) if (o) await cleanupDir(o.proj); }
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

+// last two messages ask about the whole conversation. The real server and adapter stack, the stand-in gateway, Sonnet

+// Not part of run-all: it needs the proxy. Run: node test/live-wire-switch.mjs

+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import path from 'node:path';

+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { startGatewaySim, liveBackend } = await import('./_gateway-sim.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PLAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview', PORT = 29495;
+
+const backend = await liveBackend();
+if (!backend || !(await backend.health())) { console.log('  SKIP - the development proxy is not reachable (proxy.local.json)'); process.exit(0); }
+console.log(`model behind the gateway: ${backend.model}`);
+
+const STEPS = [
+  [false, 'Create src/units.mjs exporting a function double(n) that returns twice n, and test/units.test.mjs that checks it with node:assert and prints "units tests passed". Run the test with node.'],
+  [true, 'Add a function triple(n) to the same file, with a check in the same test file. Run the test.'],
+  [true, 'Add a function half(n) the same way. Run the test.'],
+  [false, 'Add a function square(n) the same way. Run the test.'],
+  [true, 'List every function you have added in this conversation, in the order you added them. Names only, comma separated, on one line.'],
+  [false, 'Which function did you add first, and which one last? One line.'],
+];
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-wiresw-proj-'));
+await mkdir(path.join(proj, 'src'), { recursive: true });
+await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'units', type: 'module', private: true }, null, 2) + '\n');
+const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend });
+const adapter = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', maxRetries: 2 });
+const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<!doctype html><body>x</body>', baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: PLAIN, port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: PLAIN }], initialProject: proj, apiFormat: 'openai' });
+const BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const post = async (p, body) => (await fetch(BASE + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })).json().catch(() => null);
+const state = async () => (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state?limit=3000')).json();
+const runs = [];
+try {
+  for (const [on, text] of STEPS) {
+    await post('/api/agent-flags', { flag: 'sessionTransport', on });
+    const before = (await state()).events?.length ?? 0, n = gw.log.length, t0 = Date.now();
+    await post('/api/message', { text });
+    let reply = null;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 450 && reply == null; i++) {   // three minutes a message
+      await sleep(400);
+      const s = await state(); const evs = s.events || [];
+      if (evs.length > before && evs.slice(before).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text') && (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done')) { await sleep(600); const s2 = await state(); if (s2.status === 'idle' || s2.status === 'done') reply = (s2.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => e.content).pop() || ''; }
+    }
+    if (reply == null) {
+      const s = await state();
+      console.log('  STUCK: status=' + s.status + ' phase=' + s.phase + ' requests since the message=' + (gw.log.length - n) + ' ' + gw.log.slice(n).map((e) => e.status + (e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W')).join(' '));
+      for (const e of (s.events || []).slice(-8)) console.log('    ', e.type, e.verb || '', JSON.stringify(e.args || e.content || '').slice(0, 200));
+      throw new Error('the run did not finish: ' + text.slice(0, 50));
+    }
+    const reqs = gw.log.slice(n);
+    runs.push({ on, reqs, reply, ms: Date.now() - t0 });
+    console.log(`  message ${runs.length} [${on ? 'ON ' : 'off'}]: ${reqs.map((e) => (e.status !== 200 ? 'E' + e.status : e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W') + Math.round(e.readChars / 1000) + 'k').join(' ')}  (${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s)`);
+  }
+  const events = (await state()).events || [];
+  const src = await readFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'units.mjs'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+  const testRun = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['test/units.test.mjs'], { cwd: proj, encoding: 'utf8' });
+  const NAMES = ['double', 'triple', 'half', 'square'];
+  ok(NAMES.every((nm) => new RegExp('export (?:function|const) ' + nm + '\\b').test(src)), `all four functions are in src/units.mjs, one added under each setting (${NAMES.filter((nm) => src.includes(nm)).length}/4)`);
+  ok(testRun.status === 0 && /units tests passed/.test(testRun.stdout), `the project's test passes when run from here (exit ${testRun.status})`);
+  const mod = await import('file:///' + path.join(proj, 'src', 'units.mjs').replace(/\\/g, '/'));
+  ok(mod.double(7) === 14 && mod.triple(7) === 21 && mod.half(8) === 4 && mod.square(7) === 49, 'and they compute the right numbers');
+  ok(runs.filter((r) => !r.on).every((r) => r.reqs.every((e) => !e.sentSession)), 'with the switch off, nothing went on a session');
+  ok(!runs[1].reqs[0].sentSession && runs[1].reqs.slice(1).every((e) => e.sentSession), `off -> on: the first request is the whole conversation, the rest of that message rides its session (${runs[1].reqs.map((e) => (e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W')).join('')})`);
+  ok(runs[2].reqs.every((e) => e.sentSession === runs[1].reqs[0].session), `on -> on: the next message stays on that session from its first request (${runs[2].reqs.map((e) => (e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W')).join('')})`);
+  ok(!runs[4].reqs[0].sentSession && runs[4].reqs[0].session !== runs[1].reqs[0].session, 'on -> off -> on: a NEW session is opened by the whole conversation, the old one is not reused');
+  const list = String(runs[4].reply || '');
+  const order = NAMES.map((nm) => list.toLowerCase().indexOf(nm));
+  ok(order.every((i) => i >= 0) && order.every((i, k) => k === 0 || i > order[k - 1]), `asked with it ON for everything added so far, it lists all four in order - two of them were added while it was off (${list.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 110)})`);
+  ok(/double/i.test(runs[5].reply || '') && /square/i.test(runs[5].reply || ''), `asked with it OFF for the first and the last, it names both - the first was added before any session existed (${String(runs[5].reply).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 110)})`);
+  ok(gw.log.every((e) => e.status === 200), 'no request failed at the gateway');
+  ok(events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'retry' && /session/.test(JSON.stringify(e.args || {}))).length === 0, 'no session was lost');
+  const sum = (on) => runs.filter((r) => r.on === on).reduce((a, r) => a + r.reqs.reduce((x, e) => x + e.readChars, 0), 0);
+  console.log(`\n  off messages sent ${Math.round(sum(false) / 1000)}k chars in ${runs.filter((r) => !r.on).reduce((a, r) => a + r.reqs.length, 0)} requests; on messages sent ${Math.round(sum(true) / 1000)}k in ${runs.filter((r) => r.on).reduce((a, r) => a + r.reqs.length, 0)}`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await new Promise((r) => { try { server.closeAllConnections?.(); } catch {} server.close(() => r()); }); await gw.close(); await cleanupDir(proj); }
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
